    35	                let fields: BTreeMap<_,_>=table.columns.iter().filter(|(n,_)|!table.engine_field(n)).map(|(n,t)|(n,format!("{t:?};private={};constraints={:?}",table.private_field(n),table.constraints.iter().filter(|c|matches!(c,snapback4_core::schema::Constraint::Text{field,..} if field==n)).collect::<Vec<_>>()))).collect();
    36	                format!("{}:{name}:{fields:?}",if matches!(ty,Ty::Page(..)){"page"}else{"row"})
    37	            } else {format!("{ty:?}")}
    38	        }
    39	        Ty::Optional(t)=>format!("optional {}",result(t,bound)),
    40	        Ty::List(t,_)|Ty::Collection(t,_)=>format!("list {}",result(t,bound)),
    41	        Ty::Object(fields)|Ty::RowShape(fields)|Ty::RowObject(fields)=>format!("object {:?}",fields.iter().map(|(n,t)|(n,result(t,bound))).collect::<BTreeMap<_,_>>()),
    42	        Ty::Text(max)=>format!("text<={max}"),
    43	        _=>format!("{ty:?}"),
    44	    }
    45	}
    46	
    47	fn refusals(p: &BoundProgram) -> Vec<String> {
    48	    fn walk(v: &serde_json::Value, out: &mut Vec<String>) {
    49	        match v {
    50	            serde_json::Value::Object(o)=> {
    51	                if let Some(r)=o.get("Require") {out.push(format!("{}:{}",r["code"],r["message"]));}
    52	                for v in o.values(){walk(v,out);}
    53	            }
    54	            serde_json::Value::Array(a)=>for v in a{walk(v,out);},
    55	            _=>{}
    56	        }
    57	    }
    58	    let mut out=Vec::new();walk(&serde_json::to_value(&p.program.body).unwrap(),&mut out);out
    59	}
    60	
    61	/// Reports are advisory: compatibility cannot license calls or prove unchanged behavior.
    62	pub fn compare(old: &Bound, new: &Bound, label: &str) -> Vec<String> {
    63	    let mut lines=Vec::new();
    64	    for before in old.programs.iter().filter(|p|p.program.kind!=ProgramKind::Job) {
    65	        let name=&before.program.name;
    66	        let prefix=format!("old-client compatibility ({label}), operation {name}");
    67	        let Some(after)=new.programs.iter().find(|p|p.program.name==*name) else {
    68	            lines.push(format!("{prefix}: INPUT BREAK: removed operation; old calls get E_OP; keep `alias {name} = replacement` for a same-module query/mutation, or retain a wrapper"));continue;
    69	        };
    70	        if before.program.kind!=after.program.kind {lines.push(format!("{prefix}: INPUT BREAK: operation kind changed; retain the old endpoint kind"));continue;}
    71	        for (arg,kind) in &after.program.args {
    72	            if !before.program.args.contains_key(arg) && !matches!(kind,A::Optional(_)) {
    73	                lines.push(format!("{prefix}: INPUT BREAK: clients calling without new required argument {arg} get E_INPUT; give {arg} a fitting `default <literal>` or retain a compatible wrapper"));
    74	            }
    75	        }
    76	        for (arg,kind) in &before.program.args {
    77	            match after.program.args.get(arg) {
    78	                None=>lines.push(format!("{prefix}: INPUT BREAK: removed argument {arg}; old calls sending it get E_INPUT; retain `{arg}: <old type> deprecated` on a query/mutation")),
    79	                Some(next) if !accepts(kind,next)=>lines.push(format!("{prefix}: INPUT BREAK: argument {arg} retyped or narrowed ({kind:?} → {next:?}); old values may get E_INPUT; retain the old input domain or a compatible wrapper")),
    80	                _=>{}
    81	            }
    82	        }
    83	        let action_result_changed=old.schema.action_specs.get(name).map(|s|&s.returns)!=new.schema.action_specs.get(name).map(|s|&s.returns);
    84	        if action_result_changed || result(&before.result,old)!=result(&after.result,new) {lines.push(format!("{prefix}: RESULT REVIEW: result shape/value domain changed; preserve the old projection/domain or introduce a new operation before retiring old clients"));}
    85	        let old_domains=crate::result_domains::signature(before,old);
    86	        let new_domains=crate::result_domains::signature(after,new);
    87	        let changed:Vec<_>=old_domains.keys().chain(new_domains.keys()).collect::<std::collections::BTreeSet<_>>().into_iter().filter(|key|old_domains.get(*key)!=new_domains.get(*key)).cloned().collect();
    88	        if !changed.is_empty() {lines.push(format!("{prefix}: RESULT REVIEW: declared source domains consumed by this operation changed: {}; results or refusals may change even when the TypeScript type stays number/string; preserve the old domains or verify old-client handling",changed.join(", ")));}
    89	        if refusals(before)!=refusals(after) {lines.push(format!("{prefix}: BEHAVIOR REVIEW: refusal codes/messages changed ({:?} → {:?}); verify intended denials separately from input breaks",refusals(before),refusals(after)));}
    90	    }
    91	    lines
    92	}

     1	//! Conservative source-domain evidence for operation results and refusal behavior.
     2	// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.000#11-old-client-operation-contracts-rel5 — TypeScript numbers/strings must not erase consumed source-domain changes.
     3	use crate::{Bound,BoundProgram};
     4	use serde_json::Value;
     5	use std::collections::{BTreeMap,BTreeSet};
     6	
     7	// Inspect the complete operation: result construction may flow through writes,
     8	// loops, merges, helper blocks or aggregate selectors. This deliberately includes
     9	// guard/order dependencies rather than claiming a partial lineage analysis is exact.
    10	fn consumed(value:&Value,tables:&mut BTreeSet<String>,fields:&mut BTreeSet<String>) {
    11	    match value {
    12	        Value::Object(object)=> {
    13	            // Read, scan, merge lane, recursion and write targets all carry table.
    14	            if let Some(table)=object.get("table").and_then(Value::as_str) {tables.insert(table.into());}
    15	            if let Some(field)=object.get("Field") && let Some(name)=field["name"].as_str() {fields.insert(name.into());}
    16	            // Aggregate, optional-field, range and sort selectors carry field directly.
    17	            for key in ["field","next_field"] {if let Some(field)=object.get(key).and_then(Value::as_str) {fields.insert(field.into());}}
    18	            for child in object.values() {consumed(child,tables,fields);}
    19	        }
    20	        Value::Array(array)=>for child in array {consumed(child,tables,fields);},
    21	        _=>{}
    22	    }
    23	}
    24	type Definitions=BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>;
    25	fn definitions(value:&Value,defs:&mut Definitions) {
    26	    match value {
    27	        Value::Object(object)=> {
    28	            for kind in ["Let","Const","Assign"] {
    29	                if let Some(binding)=object.get(kind) && let Some(name)=binding["name"].as_str() {defs.entry(name.into()).or_default().push(binding["value"].clone());}
    30	            }
    31	            for child in object.values(){definitions(child,defs);}
    32	        }
    33	        Value::Array(array)=>for child in array{definitions(child,defs);},
    34	        _=>{}
    35	    }
    36	}
    37	fn payload_keys(value:&Value,defs:&Definitions,seen:&mut BTreeSet<String>)->Option<BTreeSet<String>> {
    38	    if let Some(object)=value.get("Object").and_then(Value::as_object) {return Some(object.keys().cloned().collect());}
    39	    if let Some(name)=value.get("Variable").and_then(Value::as_str) {
    40	        if !seen.insert(name.into()){return None;}
    41	        let mut keys=BTreeSet::new();
    42	        for value in defs.get(name)? {keys.extend(payload_keys(value,defs,&mut seen.clone())?);}
    43	        return Some(keys);
    44	    }
    45	    if let Some(shape)=value.get("Shape") {
    46	        let mut keys=payload_keys(&shape["base"],defs,seen)?;
    47	        keys.extend(shape["fields"].as_object()?.keys().cloned());return Some(keys);
    48	    }
    49	    if let Some(block)=value.get("Block") {return payload_keys(&block["value"],defs,seen);}
    50	    None
    51	}
    52	fn writes(value:&Value,defs:&Definitions,bound:&Bound,fields:&mut BTreeSet<String>) {
    53	    match value {
    54	        Value::Object(object)=> {
    55	            for (kind,payload) in [("Insert","row"),("InsertIfAbsent","row"),("Upsert","row"),("Update","patch"),("WriteTarget","patch")] {
    56	                if let Some(write)=object.get(kind) {
    57	                    if let Some(keys)=payload_keys(&write[payload],defs,&mut BTreeSet::new()) {fields.extend(keys);}
    58	                    else {
    59	                        // Opaque/spread write images conservatively consume their target domain.
    60	                        let table=if kind=="WriteTarget" {write["target"]["table"].as_str()} else {write["table"].as_str()};
    61	                        if let Some(table)=table.and_then(|name|bound.schema.table(name)) {fields.extend(table.columns.keys().cloned());}
    62	                    }
    63	                }
    64	            }
    65	            for child in object.values(){writes(child,defs,bound,fields);}
    66	        }
    67	        Value::Array(array)=>for child in array{writes(child,defs,bound,fields);},
    68	        _=>{}
    69	    }
    70	}
    71	fn row_fields(ty:&crate::types::Ty,bound:&Bound,fields:&mut BTreeSet<String>) {
    72	    use crate::types::Ty;
    73	    match ty {
    74	        Ty::Row(name)|Ty::WriteRow(name)|Ty::Page(name,_)=>if let Some(table)=bound.schema.table(name) {fields.extend(table.columns.keys().cloned());},
    75	        Ty::RowShape(parts)|Ty::RowObject(parts)=>{fields.extend(parts.keys().cloned());for ty in parts.values(){row_fields(ty,bound,fields);}},
    76	        Ty::Object(parts)=>for ty in parts.values(){row_fields(ty,bound,fields);},
    77	        Ty::Optional(ty)|Ty::List(ty,_)|Ty::Collection(ty,_)=>row_fields(ty,bound,fields),
    78	        _=>{}
    79	    }
    80	}
    81	pub(crate) fn signature(program:&BoundProgram,bound:&Bound)->BTreeMap<String,String> {
    82	    let mut tables=BTreeSet::new();let mut fields=BTreeSet::new();
    83	    let body=serde_json::to_value(&program.program.body).unwrap();
    84	    consumed(&body,&mut tables,&mut fields);
    85	    let mut defs=Definitions::new();definitions(&body,&mut defs);writes(&body,&defs,bound,&mut fields);
    86	    for read in snapback4_core::ir::read_sites(&program.program) {
    87	        if let Some(table)=bound.schema.table(&read.table) && let Some(index)=table.index(&read.index) {
    88	            // Physical key labels omit word/calendar/contribution source columns.
    89	            fields.extend(table.columns.keys().filter(|column|index.uses_column(column)).cloned());
    90	        }
    91	    }
    92	    row_fields(&program.result,bound,&mut fields);
    93	    let mut out=BTreeMap::new();
    94	    for name in tables {
    95	        let Some(table)=bound.schema.table(&name) else {continue;};
    96	        for field in &fields {
    97	            if let Some(kind)=table.columns.get(field) {
    98	                let constraints:Vec<_>=table.constraints.iter().filter(|c|matches!(c,snapback4_core::schema::Constraint::Text{field:name,..} if name==field)).collect();
    99	                out.insert(format!("{name}.{field}"),format!("{kind:?};{constraints:?}"));
   100	            }
   101	        }
   102	    }
   103	    out
   104	}

     5	fn modified(args: &[ArgDecl]) -> bool { args.iter().any(|a|a.default.is_some() || a.deprecated) }
     6	fn invalid(site: &crate::Site) -> Diagnostic {

     9	pub(super) fn expand(documents: &mut [Document], diagnostics: &mut Vec<Diagnostic>) {
    10	    for doc in documents {
    11	        for p in &doc.programs {
    12	            if (p.native || p.kind==ProgramKind::Job) && modified(&p.args) {diagnostics.push(invalid(&p.site));}
    13	        }
    14	        for a in &doc.actions {if modified(&a.args) {diagnostics.push(invalid(&a.site));}}

    17	        for (name,target,site) in &doc.aliases {
    18	            let Some(p)=authored.iter().find(|p|p.name==*target && !p.native && p.kind!=ProgramKind::Job) else {
    19	                diagnostics.push(unknown(site,format!("alias {name} needs an authored query/mutation target {target} in this module"),"target a non-native query or mutation; aliases cannot chain").guide("deploy"));continue;

    21	            let mut alias=p.clone();alias.name=name.clone();alias.site=site.clone();doc.programs.push(alias);

    23	    }

    26	pub(super) fn argument(arg: &ArgDecl, schema: &Schema) -> Fallible<(ArgType,Option<Value>)> {

    28	    let bad=||type_error(&arg.site,format!("default for {} must be a fitting non-null scalar literal and cannot combine with deprecated",arg.name),"use default <literal>, or mark the unused parameter deprecated").guide("deploy");
    29	    let value=if let Some(expr)=&arg.default {
    30	        if arg.deprecated {return Err(bad());}
    31	        let value=match expr {
    32	            Expr::Literal {value:Literal::Bool(v),..}=>Value::Bool(*v),
    33	            Expr::Literal {value:Literal::Str(v),..}=>match arg.type_.kind {TypeKind::Ref{..}|TypeKind::Id|TypeKind::Principal=>Value::Id(v.clone()),_=>Value::String(v.clone())},

    42	        match kind {ArgType::Optional(_)=>kind,_=>ArgType::Optional(Box::new(kind))}

   173	fn check_change(
   174	    dir: &Path,

   183	        let target_label = target_release.map(|n| format!("release {n}")).unwrap_or_else(|| "the new release".into());

   258	) -> Result<(), String> {
   259	    let root = dir.join("releases").join(release.to_string());

   309	    }
   310	    Ok(())
   311	}
   312	fn report(verb: &str, result: Result<(), String>) -> i32 {
   313	    // @ref LLP 3000#contended-read-serving — release completion owns no engine/lease; bound the final diagnostic drain.
   314	    // The background writer must get a chance to emit adoption estimates before
   315	    // this short-lived command exits. Broken/stalled output cannot hold exit.
   316	    let _ = snapback4_core::diagnostics::drain(std::time::Duration::from_secs(3));
   317	    match result {
   318	        Ok(()) => 0,
   319	        Err(error) => {
   320	            eprintln!("{verb}: {error}");
   321	            1
   322	        }
   323	    }
   324	}
   325	
   326	pub fn deploy(options: &Options) -> i32 {
   327	    report(
   328	        "deploy",
   329	        (|| {
   330	            let dir = canonical_directory(options)?;
   331	            let _command = command_lock(&dir)?;
   332	            let existed = dir.join("store.sqlite").exists();
   333	            let checking=options.flags.contains_key("check");
   334	            let _deployment = if checking {None} else {target(&dir)?};
   335	            if let Some(owner) = &_deployment { owner.report_reclaim(options.flags.contains_key("verbose")); }
   336	            let project = super::proof_source::project(options, "schema")?;
   337	            let sources = super::proof_source::sources(&project, options, "schema")?;
   338	            let bound = compiled_or_exit(super::proof_source::compile(&sources, options, "schema"), options.json);
   339	            let mut saved_version = super::saved_version::SavedVersion::prepare(&project,&sources,options,&dir)?;
   340	            if options.flags.contains_key("staging") {
   341	                staging::classify(&dir, _deployment.as_ref(), existed, checking)?;
   342	            }
   343	            std::fs::create_dir_all(dir.join("releases")).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
   344	            let held = if checking {
   345	                if dir.join("store.sqlite").exists() {snapback4_core::Store::open_read_only(&dir.join("store.sqlite")).and_then(|mut s|s.release_status(0,None,None)).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?}
   346	                else {serde_json::json!({"release":null,"revision":0,"generation":1})}
   347	            } else {status(&dir, _deployment.as_ref(), 0, None, None)?};
   348	            let previous = if let Some(owner) = _deployment.as_ref() {
   349	                current(&dir, owner)?
   350	            } else {
   351	                held["release"].as_u64()
   352	            };
   353	            let revision = held["revision"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0);
   354	            check_change(&dir, previous, &bound, options, None)?;
   355	            let releases = dir.join("releases").canonicalize().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
   356	            let stage = releases.join(format!(".stage-{}", std::process::id()));
   357	            if stage.exists() {std::fs::remove_dir_all(&stage).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;}
   358	            std::fs::create_dir(&stage).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
   359	            let _cleanup=Stage(stage.clone());
   360	            std::fs::create_dir(stage.join("snapback")).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
   361	            let source_hash = {
   362	                use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};
   363	                let mut hash = Sha256::new();
   364	                let mut ordered = sources.iter().collect::<Vec<_>>();
   365	                ordered.sort_by(|a,b| a.0.cmp(&b.0));
   366	                for (name,text) in ordered {
   367	                    hash.update((name.len() as u64).to_le_bytes()); hash.update(name.as_bytes());
   368	                    hash.update((text.len() as u64).to_le_bytes()); hash.update(text.as_bytes());
   369	                }
   370	                format!("{:x}", hash.finalize())
   371	            };
   372	            for (name,text) in sources {
   373	                let path=stage.join("snapback").join(name);
   374	                if let Some(parent)=path.parent(){std::fs::create_dir_all(parent).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;}
   375	                std::fs::write(path,text).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
   376	            }
   377	            crate::dev_worker::package(&project, &stage)?;
   378	            // @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000.000#2 — saved schemas prepare companion types in a private snapshot, never in working generated/.
   379	            let private = if options.flags.contains_key("schema") || crate::dev_worker::source(&project).is_some() {
   380	                Some(super::proof_source::Candidate::prepare(&project, &stage, &bound)?)
   381	            } else { None };
   382	            let build_project = private.as_ref().map(|candidate| &candidate.project).unwrap_or(&project);
   383	            // A native job's module travels with the captured source.
   384	            if let Some(built) =
   385	                crate::native::build(build_project, &bound).map_err(|p| p.to_string())?
   386	            {
   387	                let generated = stage.join("snapback").join("generated");
   388	                std::fs::create_dir_all(&generated).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
   389	                std::fs::copy(&built.path, generated.join("native.wasm"))
   390	                    .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
   391	            }
   392	            std::fs::write(stage.join("device-compatibility.json"), serde_json::to_vec_pretty(&snapback4_core::device_protocol::compatibility(&bound.schema.public_projection())).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
   393	            crate::dev_worker::package_entry(build_project, &stage)?;
   394	            if let Some(previous)=previous {
   395	                if held["release"].as_u64()==Some(previous) && crate::release_files::digest(&stage)?==crate::release_files::digest(&releases.join(previous.to_string()))? {
   396	                    std::fs::remove_dir_all(&stage).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
   397	                    if let Some(saved) = &mut saved_version { saved.finish(previous,&source_hash)?; }
   398	                    if options.json {println!("{}",serde_json::json!({"unchanged":true,"release":previous,"generation":held["generation"]}));}
   399	                    else {println!("unchanged; release {previous} stays current as generation {}",held["generation"]);}
   400	                    return Ok(());
   401	                }
   402	            }
   403	            // @ref LLP 3000.000.000#16-release-reactivation-and-rollback-foresight-rel5 — equal retained executable bytes select their existing identity.
   404	            let staged_digest = crate::release_files::digest(&stage)?;
   405	            let mut matching = std::fs::read_dir(&releases).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?
   406	                .filter_map(Result::ok).filter_map(|e| e.file_name().to_str()?.parse::<u64>().ok())
   407	                .filter(|n| selectable(&dir, *n).is_ok())
   408	                .collect::<Vec<_>>();
   409	            matching.sort_unstable_by(|a,b| b.cmp(a));
   410	            let mut matched = None;
   411	            for n in matching {
   412	                if crate::release_files::digest(&releases.join(n.to_string()))? == staged_digest { matched=Some(n); break; }
   413	            }
   414	            if let Some(n) = matched {
   415	                let mut preview_options=options.clone();
   416	                preview_options.flags.insert("deploy-direction".into(),String::new());
   417	                preview_options.flags.insert("restore-dormant".into(),String::new());
   418	                if !checking { preview_options.flags.insert("validation-only".into(),String::new()); }
   419	                super::release_preview::preview(&dir.join("store.sqlite"),releases.join(n.to_string()),n,&preview_options)?;
   420	                if checking { return Ok(()); }
   421	                let selected = bound_at(releases.join(n.to_string()))?;
   422	                activate(&dir,_deployment.as_ref(),n,previous,revision,selected,false,true,options.flags.contains_key("allow-feed-adoption"),options,None)?;
   423	                if let Some(saved)=&mut saved_version {saved.finish(n,&source_hash)?;}
   424	                return Ok(());
   425	            }
   426	            let mut n=std::fs::read_dir(&releases).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.filter_map(Result::ok).filter_map(|e|e.file_name().to_str()?.parse::<u64>().ok()).max().unwrap_or(0);
   427	            let staged_bound = bound_at(stage.clone())?;
   428	            if options.flags.contains_key("check") {
   429	                let mut preview_options=options.clone();preview_options.flags.insert("deploy-direction".into(),String::new());
   430	                let result=super::release_preview::preview(&dir.join("store.sqlite"),stage.clone(),n.checked_add(1).ok_or("release identity exhausted")?,&preview_options);
   431	                let _=std::fs::remove_dir_all(&stage);
   432	                return result;
   433	            }
   434	            let mut validation=options.clone();
   435	            validation.flags.insert("deploy-direction".into(),String::new());
   436	            validation.flags.insert("validation-only".into(),String::new());
   437	            super::release_preview::preview(&dir.join("store.sqlite"),stage.clone(),n.checked_add(1).ok_or("release identity exhausted")?,&validation)?;
   438	            if staged_bound.schema != bound.schema { return Err("staged source does not match the compiled schema".into()); }
   439	            n=n.checked_add(1).ok_or("release identity exhausted")?;
   440	            let final_dir=releases.join(n.to_string());
   441	            // @ref LLP 3000.000.000#10-wave-10-release-lifecycle-amendment — only committed adoption makes a candidate selectable.
   442	            std::fs::write(stage.join(".pending-adoption"), b"pending\n").map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
   443	            if staged_bound.schema != bound.schema {
   444	                return Err("staged source does not match the compiled schema".into());
   445	            }
   446	            // @ref LLP 3000.000.000.000.000#4-cli-surface-contracts — provenance is immutable metadata, excluded from executable identity so identical deploys stay no-ops.
   447	            std::fs::write(stage.join("provenance.json"), format!("{}\n", serde_json::json!({
   448	                "release": n, "sourceHash": source_hash, "cliVersion": env!("CARGO_PKG_VERSION"), "createdAt": snapback4_core::now_ms()
   449	            }))).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
   450	            crate::release_files::durable(&stage)?;
   451	            std::fs::rename(&stage, &final_dir).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
   452	            let mut candidate=crate::release_files::Candidate::new(&dir,n);
   453	            std::fs::File::open(&releases)
   454	                .and_then(|f| f.sync_all())

   457	                &dir,
   458	                _deployment.as_ref(),
   459	                n,
   460	                previous,
   461	                revision,
   462	                staged_bound,
   463	                false,
   464	                options.flags.contains_key("restore-dormant"),
   465	                options.flags.contains_key("allow-feed-adoption"),
   466	                options,
   467	                Some(&mut candidate),
   468	            );
   469	            match result {
   470	                Ok(()) => {
   471	                    candidate.committed=true;
   472	                    if let Err(error)=std::fs::remove_file(final_dir.join(".pending-adoption")) {eprintln!("release {n} committed; pending-marker cleanup failed: {error}; history proves adoption");}
   473	                    if let Some(saved) = &mut saved_version {saved.finish(n,&source_hash).map_err(|e|format!("release {n} committed, but saving its snapshot failed: {e}; retry unchanged deploy with --save-version"))?;}
   474	                }
   475	                Err(error) => {
   476	                    if options.json && error.contains("dormant names") {
   477	                        let mut inventory=Vec::new();let mut cursor=None;
   478	                        loop {
   479	                            let page=status(&dir,_deployment.as_ref(),0,cursor,None)?;
   480	                            let items=page["items"].as_array().ok_or("invalid dormant inventory response")?;
   481	                            if items.is_empty() {break;}
   482	                            inventory.extend(items.iter().cloned());
   483	                            cursor=items.last().and_then(|i|i["key"].as_str()).map(str::to_owned);
   484	                            if cursor.is_none() {break;}
   485	                        }
   486	                        println!("{}",serde_json::json!({"ok":false,"code":"E_ADOPT","message":error,"dormant":inventory}));
   487	                    }
   488	                    return Err(error);
   489	                }
   490	            }
   491	            Ok(())
   492	        })(),
   493	    )
   494	}
   495	
   496	pub fn rollback(options: &Options) -> i32 {
   497	    report(
   498	        "rollback",
   499	        (|| {
   500	            let dir = existing_deployment(&directory(options)?)?;
   501	            let _command = command_lock(&dir)?;
   502	            let _deployment = target(&dir)?;
   503	            if let Some(owner) = &_deployment { owner.report_reclaim(options.flags.contains_key("verbose")); }
   504	            let held = status(&dir, _deployment.as_ref(), 0, None, None)?;
   505	            let active = if let Some(owner) = _deployment.as_ref() {
   506	                current(&dir, owner)?
   507	            } else {
   508	                held["release"].as_u64()
   509	            }
   510	            .ok_or("no current release")?;
   511	            let revision = held["revision"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0);
   512	            let selected = options
   513	                .flags
   514	                .get("to")
   515	                .map(|n| {
   516	                    if n=="latest" {return latest(&dir).map_err(|_| "no complete latest release");}
   517	                    n.parse::<u64>()
   518	                        .ok()
   519	                        .filter(|n| *n > 0)
   520	                        .ok_or("--to requires a positive immutable release id")
   521	                })
   522	                .transpose()?;
   523	            let previous = selected
   524	                .or_else(|| held["previous_active"].as_u64())
   525	                .ok_or("nothing to roll back to")?;

     1	//! @ref LLP 3000.000.000#16-release-reactivation-and-rollback-foresight-rel5 — future-write risks are distinct from snapshot validation.
     2	use snapback4_core::{Schema, successor::{self, Change}, schema::ColumnDefault};
     3	use std::path::Path;
     4	
     5	pub(super) fn risks(candidate: &Schema, old: &Schema, release: u64, dir: &Path) -> Vec<String> {
     6	    let mut risks:Vec<_>=successor::classify(candidate,old).into_iter().filter_map(|step| {
     7	        let path=step.path.strip_prefix("tables.")?;
     8	        let (table,column)=path.split_once(".columns.")?;
     9	        let target=old.tables.get(table)?;
    10	        if target.engine_field(column) || old.maintain_for(table).is_some() || target.maintained.contains_key(column) {return None;}
    11	        let before=target.columns.get(column)?;
    12	        let from=candidate.tables.get(table)?;
    13	        let reason=match from.columns.get(column) {
    14	            None if !before.is_optional() && !matches!(target.defaults.get(column),Some(ColumnDefault::Literal(_))) =>
    15	                "new rows will lack the old required field, so rollback can refuse".to_owned(),
    16	            Some(kind) if kind!=before && matches!(step.change,Change::Narrowing|Change::Destructive) =>
    17	                format!("new writes allowed by {kind} may not fit {before}, so rollback can refuse or hide rows"),
    18	            _ => return None,
    19	        };
    20	        Some(format!("rollback foresight: release {release}, {table}.{column}: {reason}; keep an expand/contract bridge with the old required fields and compatible domains; run `snapback4 rollback {} --to {release} --check --bridge <new-dir>` after writes",dir.display()))
    21	    }).collect();
    22	    for (name,target) in old.visible_tables().filter(|(_,t)|t.ephemeral.is_none()) {
    23	        let Some(from)=candidate.tables.get(name) else {continue;};
    24	        if old.maintain_for(name).is_some() {continue;}
    25	        for (index,definition) in &target.indexes {
    26	            if definition.unique && from.indexes.get(index)!=Some(definition) {
    27	                risks.push(format!("rollback foresight: release {release}, unique index {name}.{index}: future writes may introduce duplicate keys and refuse restoration; keep the old uniqueness in a bridge; run `snapback4 rollback {} --to {release} --check --bridge <new-dir>` after writes",dir.display()));
    28	            }
    29	        }
    30	        if target.constraints.iter().any(|c|!from.constraints.contains(c) && matches!(c,snapback4_core::schema::Constraint::Text{..}|snapback4_core::schema::Constraint::Cap{..}|snapback4_core::schema::Constraint::FinalExistsOnInsert{..})) {
    31	            risks.push(format!("rollback foresight: release {release}, table {name}: changed constraints may let future writes refuse rollback; preserve old bounds/quotas in a bridge; run `snapback4 rollback {} --to {release} --check --bridge <new-dir>` after writes",dir.display()));
    32	        }
    33	    }
    34	    risks
    35	}

     1	//! Operator preflight runs the real activation on a private disk-backed snapshot.
     2	// REL3 owns dormant-visibility counts here; SURF clause narration lives in release_narration.
     3	// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#92-exact-preview-and-operator-vocabulary — no live pointer, generation or audit changes.
     4	use super::*;
     5	use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
     6	struct Snapshot(PathBuf);
     7	impl Drop for Snapshot {
     8	    fn drop(&mut self) {
     9	        let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&self.0);
    10	    }
    11	}
    12	
    13	pub(super) fn preview(
    14	    store_path: &Path,
    15	    root: PathBuf,
    16	    release: u64,
    17	    options: &Options,
    18	) -> Result<(), String> {
    19	    let bound = super::releases::bound_at(root.clone())?;
    20	    let project = Project { root };
    21	    let native = crate::native::prepare(&project, &bound).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    22	    crate::dev_worker::prepare_release(&project,&bound.schema)?;
    23	    let id = snapback4_core::ids::mint(snapback4_core::now_ms(), &mut snapback4_core::ids::Mint::default())
    24	        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    25	    let snapshot = Snapshot(std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("snapback4-release-preview-{id}")));
    26	    snapback4_core::private_fs::directory(&snapshot.0).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    27	    let path = snapshot.0.join("store.sqlite");
    28	    // SQLite VACUUM INTO takes a coherent read snapshot even with a live WAL writer.
    29	    // Busy/disk errors refuse this preview; never fall back to copying database bytes.
    30	    if store_path.exists() {
    31	    let source = snapback4_core::Store::open_read_only(store_path).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    32	    source
    33	        .conn()
    34	        .execute("VACUUM INTO ?1", [path.to_string_lossy().as_ref()])
    35	        .map_err(|e| {
    36	            format!("snapshot preview failed (retry after store/disk becomes available): {e}")
    37	        })?;

+// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.000#11-old-client-operation-contracts-rel5 — the installed guide is the source exercised by packed qualification.
+import {readFileSync, writeFileSync, rmSync} from 'node:fs';
+import {join} from 'node:path';
+import {runToolchainCommand} from './publication-starters.mjs';
+export async function proveReleaseTransitions(cwd) {
+  const path=join(cwd,'release-transitions.mjs');
+  const source=readFileSync(new URL('./release-transitions-cases.mjs',import.meta.url),'utf8');
+  writeFileSync(path,source+`\nimport {cli,startServe} from 'snapback4/proof';\nconsole.log(JSON.stringify(await releaseTransitions({cli,startServe},process.cwd())));\n`);
+  const env={...process.env};delete env.SNAPBACK4_BIN;delete env.PORT_LO;delete env.PORT_HI;
+  try {await runToolchainCommand(process.execPath,[path],{cwd,env,timeout:240_000});}
+  finally {rmSync(path,{force:true});}
+  return {status:'passed',cases:['reactivation','refused-deploy-cleanliness','rollback-foresight','old-client-diff','installed-v1-v2-guide']};
+}

index 0000000000..b0bdfd4be7

+// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#16-release-reactivation-and-rollback-foresight-rel5 — run the same transitions with development and installed binaries.
+import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
+import { mkdirSync, writeFileSync, readFileSync, readdirSync, mkdtempSync, rmSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { join } from 'node:path';
+
+export async function releaseTransitions({ cli, startServe }, parent) {
+  const root=mkdtempSync(join(parent,'release-transitions-'));
+  const active=new Set();
+  try {
+    const fixture=name=> {
+      const cwd=join(root,name);mkdirSync(join(cwd,'snapback'),{recursive:true});
+      const deployment=join(cwd,'deployment'),schema=join(cwd,'snapback/schema.q');
+      const invoke=args=>cli(args,{cwd,timeoutMs:60_000});
+      const run=async args=>{const r=await invoke(args);assert.equal(r.code,0,r.stdout+r.stderr);return r;};
+      const status=async()=>JSON.parse((await run(['releases',deployment,'--json'])).stdout);
+      const releases=()=>readdirSync(join(deployment,'releases')).sort();
+      const source=text=>writeFileSync(schema,text);
+      const serve=async()=> {
+        const host=await startServe(deployment,{cwd,timeoutMs:60_000});active.add(host);
+        const signed=await (await fetch(host.url+'/auth/guest',{method:'POST',body:'{}'})).json();
+        assert.ok(signed.session?.token,JSON.stringify(signed));
+        const headers={'content-type':'application/json',authorization:'Bearer '+signed.session.token};
+        const post=async(path,args={},id)=> {
+          const r=await fetch(host.url+path,{method:'POST',headers,body:JSON.stringify({args,...(id?{id}:{})}),signal:AbortSignal.timeout(30_000)});
+          return r.json();
+        };
+        return {host,post};
+      };
+      return {cwd,deployment,schema,invoke,run,status,releases,source,serve};
+    };
+    const f=fixture('reactivation');
+    const v1=`use identity guests
+table items:
+  body: text <=40
+  public 'release proof'
+  insert <- allow
+  update <- allow
+mutation add(body: text <=40):
+  return insert items {body}
+query list():
+  return items first 20 by .id
+`;
+    const v2=v1.replace('  body: text','  extra: text <=40 default \'default\'\n  body: text')+`mutation mark(id: items):
+  return update items[id] {extra: 'preserved'}
+`;
+    f.source(v1);await f.run(['deploy',f.deployment]);
+    f.source(v2);await f.run(['deploy',f.deployment]);await f.run(['rollback',f.deployment,'--to','1']);
+    const before=await f.status();
+    const check=JSON.parse((await f.run(['deploy',f.deployment,'--check','--json'])).stdout);
+    assert.equal(check.plan.release,2);assert.deepEqual(await f.status(),before);assert.deepEqual(f.releases(),['1','2']);
+    assert.equal(JSON.parse((await f.run(['deploy',f.deployment,'--json'])).stdout).release,2);
+    const {host,post}=await f.serve();
+    const added=await post('/m/add',{body:'kept'},'rel5-add');assert.equal(added.state,'sent',JSON.stringify(added));
+    const id=added.result.id;
+    assert.equal((await post('/m/mark',{id},'rel5-mark')).state,'sent');
+    await f.run(['rollback',f.deployment,'--to','1','--restore-dormant']);
+    await f.run(['deploy',f.deployment]);
+    assert.equal((await f.status()).release,2);assert.equal((await post('/q/list')).data[0].extra,'preserved');
+    const unchanged=await f.status();assert.equal(JSON.parse((await f.run(['deploy',f.deployment,'--json'])).stdout).unchanged,true);
+    assert.deepEqual(await f.status(),unchanged);
+    f.source(v2.replace('  body: text','  required: int\n  body: text').replace('insert items {body}','insert items {body, required: 1}'));
+    const failed=await f.invoke(['deploy',f.deployment]);assert.equal(failed.code,1);assert.match(failed.stderr,/E_ADOPT/);
+    assert.deepEqual(f.releases(),['1','2']);assert.deepEqual(await f.status(),unchanged);
+    assert.equal((await post('/q/list')).data[0].extra,'preserved');
+    await host.stop();active.delete(host);
+    // Failure after validation, before database commit, also cleans its numeric source.
+    f.source(v2.replace("return items first 20", "return items first 19"));
+    rmSync(join(f.deployment,'current'));mkdirSync(join(f.deployment,'current'));
+    const pointerFailure=await f.invoke(['deploy',f.deployment]);assert.equal(pointerFailure.code,1);assert.match(pointerFailure.stderr,/current must be a regular/);
+    assert.deepEqual(f.releases(),['1','2']);rmSync(join(f.deployment,'current'),{recursive:true});writeFileSync(join(f.deployment,'current'),'2\n');
+
+    const g=fixture('foresight');
+    const prior=`use identity guests
+table gauges:
+  minPressure: int 0..2000
+  public 'rollback proof'
+  insert <- allow
+mutation add():
+  return insert gauges {minPressure: 4}
+`;
+    const next=prior.replace('  minPressure: int 0..2000\n','  lowPressure: int 0..2000 default 0\n').replace('{minPressure: 4}','{lowPressure: 4}');
+    g.source(prior);await g.run(['deploy',g.deployment]);g.source(next);
+    const foresight=JSON.parse((await g.run(['deploy',g.deployment,'--check','--json'])).stdout);
+    assert.ok(foresight.hazards.some(s=>s.includes('gauges.minPressure')&&s.includes('new rows')&&s.includes('--to 1 --check --bridge')));
+    assert.match((await g.run(['deploy',g.deployment])).stderr,/rollback foresight.*gauges.minPressure/);
+    const h=await g.serve();assert.equal((await h.post('/m/add',{},'rel5-new-gauge')).state,'sent');
+    const rollback=await g.invoke(['rollback',g.deployment,'--to','1','--check']);assert.equal(rollback.code,1);assert.match(rollback.stderr,/minPressure/);
+    await h.host.stop();active.delete(h.host);
+
+    const c=fixture('contracts');
+    // Execute both Quarry blocks from the installed guide, without source substitutions.
+    const guide=(await c.run(['guide','deploy','--section','Keep v1 clients working'])).stdout;
+    const blocks=[...guide.matchAll(/```quarry\n([\s\S]*?)```/g)].map(m=>m[1]);assert.equal(blocks.length,2,guide);
+    c.source(blocks[0]);await c.run(['deploy',c.deployment]);
+    const oldHost=await c.serve();const frozenArgs={name:'Ada',obsolete:'old input'};
+    assert.deepEqual((await oldHost.post('/q/greet',frozenArgs)).data,{message:'Ada!'});
+    c.source(blocks[1]);
+    const safe=JSON.parse((await c.run(['deploy',c.deployment,'--check','--json'])).stdout);assert.deepEqual(safe.clientCompatibility,[]);
+    await c.run(['deploy',c.deployment]);
+    assert.deepEqual((await oldHost.post('/q/greet',frozenArgs)).data,{message:'Ada!'});
+    assert.deepEqual((await oldHost.post('/q/greeting',{name:'Ada'})).data,{message:'Ada!'});
+    await oldHost.host.stop();active.delete(oldHost.host);
+    const unsafe=blocks[1].replace("suffix: text <=1 default '!'","suffix: text <=1").replace(', obsolete: text <=40 deprecated','');
+    c.source(unsafe);
+    const diff=JSON.parse((await c.run(['deploy',c.deployment,'--check','--json'])).stdout).clientCompatibility.join('\n');
+    assert.match(diff,/greet.*argument suffix retyped or narrowed/);assert.match(diff,/greet.*removed argument obsolete/);
+    mkdirSync(join(c.cwd,'versions/v1'),{recursive:true});writeFileSync(join(c.cwd,'versions/v1/schema.q'),blocks[0]);
+    writeFileSync(join(c.cwd,'snapback/client-compatibility.json'),JSON.stringify({baseline:'versions/v1',since_release:2}));
+    const baseline=await c.run(['check']);assert.match(baseline.stderr,/old-client compatibility \(baseline .*new required argument suffix/);
+    const generated=readFileSync(join(c.cwd,'snapback/generated/api.ts'),'utf8');assert.match(generated,/suffix: string/);assert.doesNotMatch(generated,/suffix\?:/);
+    const coldRollback=await c.run(['rollback',c.deployment,'--to','1']);
+    assert.match(coldRollback.stderr,/old-client compatibility.*release 2.*greeting.*removed operation/);
+    const hotRollback=await c.serve();
+    await c.run(['rollback',c.deployment,'--to','2']);
+    const hotWarning=await c.run(['rollback',c.deployment,'--to','1']);
+    assert.match(hotWarning.stderr,/old-client compatibility.*release 2.*greeting.*removed operation/);
+    await hotRollback.host.stop();active.delete(hotRollback.host);
+    const d=fixture('projected-domains');
+    const rows=`use identity guests
+table items:
+  n: int 0..10
+  label: text 1..20
+  public 'result domain proof'
+query list():
+  return items first 10 by .id {n, label}
+`;
+    d.source(rows);await d.run(['deploy',d.deployment]);
+    d.source(rows.replace('int 0..10','int 0..20').replace('text 1..20','text 0..20'));
+    const domains=JSON.parse((await d.run(['deploy',d.deployment,'--check','--json'])).stdout).clientCompatibility.join('\n');
+    assert.match(domains,/RESULT REVIEW.*items.label/);assert.match(domains,/RESULT REVIEW.*items.n/);
+    return {status:'passed',reactivation:['cold','hot','same-id','dormant-values','unchanged'],refusals:['adoption','post-validation-pointer','no-candidate-left'],rollbackForesight:'dropped-required-column-and-later-writes',oldClients:'installed-guide-signature-diff-baseline-projected-domains'};
+  } finally {
+    try {for(const host of active)await host.stop();} finally {rmSync(root,{recursive:true,force:true});}
+  }
+}

pub enum Stmt {
    Const { name: String, value: Expr },
    Let { name: String, value: Expr },
    Assign { name: String, value: Expr },
    Push { target: String, value: Expr },
    If { condition: Expr, then_body: Vec<Stmt>, else_body: Vec<Stmt> },
    For { binding: String, domain: LoopDomain, body: Vec<Stmt> },
    Require {
        condition: Expr,
        code: String,
        #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
        site: Option<String>,
        /// The author's text for the refusal (`require … else CODE 'message'`); the code stays the contract.
        #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
        message: Option<String>,
    },
    /// A false predicate rolls the transaction back and re-reads `current`.
    Check { condition: Expr, current: Read },
    /// A unique clash yields absence, never the conflicting row.
    InsertIfAbsent { table: String, row: Expr, result: String },
    Insert { table: String, row: Expr },
    Update { table: String, key: Expr, patch: Expr },
    Upsert { table: String, key: Expr, row: Expr },
    /// Target lookup and the old image stay inside the engine. Only a read-filtered result is bound.
    WriteTarget { target: Read, patch: Expr, upsert: bool, condition: Option<WriteCondition>, result: Option<String> },
    Delete { table: String, key: Expr },
    /// Alternate-key delete resolves inside the engine before charging hidden expiry work.
    DeleteTarget { target: Read },
    Emit { effect: String, payload: Expr, #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")] once: Option<Expr> },
    Return(Expr),
    Break,
    Continue,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct WriteCondition {
    pub old: String,
    pub setup: Vec<Stmt>,
    pub value: Expr,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum LoopDomain {
    Page(Scan),
    LiteralRange { start: i64, end_exclusive: i64 },
    ExpressionRange { start: Expr, end_exclusive: Expr },
    ArgList { name: String, max: u32 },
    DerivedArray { value: Expr, max: u32, bound_source: String },
    Recurse(Recurse),
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Recurse {
    pub site: String,
    pub table: String,
    pub index: String,
    pub seed: Box<Expr>,
    pub next_field: String,
    pub depth: u32,
    pub fanout: u32,
    pub provenance: KeyProvenance,
}

pub const MAX_RECURSE_DEPTH: u32 = 10_000;

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum Expr {
    Literal(Value),
    Variable(String),
    Input(Input),
    Field { base: Box<Expr>, name: String },
    Array(Vec<Expr>),
    Object(BTreeMap<String, Expr>),
    Shape { base: Box<Expr>, fields: BTreeMap<String, Expr> },
    Binary { op: BinaryOp, left: Box<Expr>, right: Box<Expr> },
    Unary { op: UnaryOp, value: Box<Expr> },
    Builtin { builtin: Builtin, args: Vec<Expr> },
    Get(Read),
    Exists(Read),
    Block { body: Vec<Stmt>, value: Box<Expr> },
    Scan(Scan),
    Merge(Merge),
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum Input {

    Now,
    NewId,
    ReadTime,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum BinaryOp {

    SameValueZero,
    Lt,

    Gt,

    Add,
    Sub,
    Mul,
    And,

}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum UnaryOp {
    Not,
    Neg,
}
    Divide {
        rounding: Rounding,
        /// Decimal places requested by the four-argument surface. `None`
        /// preserves the dividend's already-scaled representation.
        #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
        scale: Option<u8>,
    },
    Sort { keys: Vec<SortKey> },
    MentionTokens,
    SearchPresent,
    /// A query row assertion cannot classify a metered-out point as absent.
    RequireUncapped,
    DeterministicShare,
    Count,
    Sum,
    CountCapped,
    /// `connected[p]`: how many connections the principal holds open on the
    /// server now; `exists connected[p]` is `> 0`. A device answers 1.
    Connected,
}

impl Builtin {
    pub fn arity(&self) -> usize {
        match self {
            #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
            Self::JobQuery { .. } => 1,
            #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
            Self::JobAs => 2,
            Self::ReceiptSettled { .. } | Self::Extremum { .. } | Self::ProjectFields { .. } | Self::OptionalField { .. } | Self::Calendar { .. } | Self::Civil(_) | Self::Group { .. } | Self::First | Self::Last | Self::Len | Self::Lower | Self::Trim | Self::Sha256 | Self::Sort { .. } | Self::MentionTokens | Self::SearchPresent | Self::RequireUncapped | Self::Count | Self::Sum | Self::CountCapped | Self::Connected => 1,
            Self::ExtremumOf { .. } | Self::At | Self::Concat | Self::Join | Self::Min | Self::Max | Self::Divmod { .. } | Self::Divide { .. } => 2,
            Self::Slice | Self::DeterministicShare => 3,
pub fn read_sites(program: &Program) -> Vec<ReadSite> {
    let mut out = Vec::new();
    stmts_read_sites(&program.body, &mut out);
    out
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
pub struct ReadSite {
    pub site: String,
    pub table: String,
    pub index: String,
    /// `point`, `scan`, or `walk`.
    pub kind: &'static str,
    pub take: u64,
}

fn stmts_read_sites(stmts: &[Stmt], out: &mut Vec<ReadSite>) {
    for stmt in stmts {
        match stmt {
            Stmt::Const { value, .. } | Stmt::Let { value, .. } | Stmt::Assign { value, .. } | Stmt::Push { value, .. } | Stmt::Return(value) => expr_read_sites(value, out),
            Stmt::Emit { payload: value, once, .. } => { expr_read_sites(value, out); if let Some(value) = once { expr_read_sites(value, out); } },
            Stmt::If { condition, then_body, else_body } => {
                expr_read_sites(condition, out);
                stmts_read_sites(then_body, out);
                stmts_read_sites(else_body, out);
            }
            Stmt::For { domain, body, .. } => {
                match domain {
                    LoopDomain::Page(scan) => scan_read_sites(scan, out),
                    LoopDomain::ExpressionRange { start, end_exclusive } => { expr_read_sites(start, out); expr_read_sites(end_exclusive, out); }
                    LoopDomain::DerivedArray { value, .. } => expr_read_sites(value, out),
                    LoopDomain::Recurse(recurse) => {
                        expr_read_sites(&recurse.seed, out);
                        out.push(ReadSite { site: recurse.site.clone(), table: recurse.table.clone(), index: recurse.index.clone(), kind: "walk", take: recurse.depth as u64 * recurse.fanout.max(1) as u64 });
                    }
                    LoopDomain::LiteralRange { .. } | LoopDomain::ArgList { .. } => {}
                }
                stmts_read_sites(body, out);
            }
            Stmt::Require { condition, .. } => expr_read_sites(condition, out),
            Stmt::Check { condition, current } => {
                expr_read_sites(condition, out);
                out.push(ReadSite { site: current.site.clone(), table: current.table.clone(), index: current.index.clone(), kind: "point", take: 1 });
            }
            Stmt::InsertIfAbsent { row, .. } | Stmt::Insert { row, .. } => expr_read_sites(row, out),
            Stmt::Update { key, patch, .. } => {
                expr_read_sites(key, out);
                expr_read_sites(patch, out);
            }
            Stmt::Upsert { key, row, .. } => {
                expr_read_sites(key, out);
                expr_read_sites(row, out);
            }
            Stmt::WriteTarget { target, patch, condition, .. } => {
                out.push(ReadSite { site: target.site.clone(), table: target.table.clone(), index: target.index.clone(), kind: "write target", take: 1 });
                for key in &target.key { expr_read_sites(key, out); }
                expr_read_sites(patch, out);
                if let Some(condition) = condition { stmts_read_sites(&condition.setup, out); expr_read_sites(&condition.value, out); }
            }
            Stmt::Delete { key, .. } => expr_read_sites(key, out),
            Stmt::DeleteTarget { target } => {
                out.push(ReadSite { site: target.site.clone(), table: target.table.clone(), index: target.index.clone(), kind: "write target", take: 1 });
                for key in &target.key { expr_read_sites(key, out); }
            }
            Stmt::Break | Stmt::Continue => {}
        }
    }
}

fn scan_read_sites(scan: &Scan, out: &mut Vec<ReadSite>) {
    if let Some(filter) = &scan.filter { expr_read_sites(filter, out); }
    if let Some(range) = &scan.range {
        for bound in [&range.gt, &range.gte, &range.lt, &range.lte].into_iter().flatten() { expr_read_sites(bound, out); }
    }
    for key in &scan.prefix {
        // @ref LLP 3000.007#18.1 — matching expressions can read independent sources; retain them in the execution facts.
        match key { IndexKeyExpr::Scalar(expr) | IndexKeyExpr::WordPrefix(expr) => expr_read_sites(expr, out) }
    }
    for cursor in [&scan.after, &scan.before].into_iter().flatten() { expr_read_sites(cursor, out); }
    out.push(ReadSite { site: scan.site.clone(), table: scan.table.clone(), index: scan.index.clone(), kind: "scan", take: scan.take.maximum() });
}

fn expr_read_sites(expr: &Expr, out: &mut Vec<ReadSite>) {
    match expr {
        Expr::Literal(_) | Expr::Variable(_) | Expr::Input(_) => {}
        Expr::Field { base, .. } => expr_read_sites(base, out),
        Expr::Array(items) => items.iter().for_each(|item| expr_read_sites(item, out)),
        Expr::Object(fields) => fields.values().for_each(|item| expr_read_sites(item, out)),
        Expr::Shape { base, fields } => { expr_read_sites(base, out); fields.values().for_each(|item| expr_read_sites(item, out)); }
        Expr::Binary { left, right, .. } => {
            expr_read_sites(left, out);
            expr_read_sites(right, out);
        }
        Expr::Unary { value, .. } => expr_read_sites(value, out),
        Expr::Builtin { args, .. } => args.iter().for_each(|item| expr_read_sites(item, out)),
        Expr::Get(read) | Expr::Exists(read) => {
            read.key.iter().for_each(|item| expr_read_sites(item, out));
            out.push(ReadSite { site: read.site.clone(), table: read.table.clone(), index: read.index.clone(), kind: "point", take: 1 });
        }
        Expr::Block { body, value } => { stmts_read_sites(body, out); expr_read_sites(value, out); }
        Expr::Scan(scan) => scan_read_sites(scan, out),
        Expr::Merge(merge) => {
            if let Some(after) = merge.position.as_ref().and_then(|position| position.after.as_ref()) { expr_read_sites(after, out); }
            for lane in &merge.lanes {
                if let Some(guard) = &lane.guard { expr_read_sites(guard, out); }
                if let Some(project) = &lane.project { expr_read_sites(project, out); }
                if let Some(value) = &lane.distinct { expr_read_sites(value, out); }
                stmts_read_sites(&lane.setup, out);
                if let Some(source) = &lane.source {
                    if let Some(scan) = source.scan() { scan_read_sites(scan, out); }
                    for value in source.expressions() { expr_read_sites(value, out); }
                }
                scan_read_sites(&lane.scan, out);
            }
        }
    }
}

        (Some(min), Some(max)) => format!("int {min}..{max}"),
        (Some(min), None) => format!("int {min}.."),
        (None, Some(max)) => format!("int ..{max}"),
        (None, None) => "int".into(),
    }
}

// @ref LLP 3000.040#13-representation — an unwritten end is the i53 limit itself, so the
// variant stays as small as `Int`'s neighbours (no Option) and the bytes omit it.
pub fn int_floor() -> i64 { -crate::value::I53_MAX }
pub fn int_ceiling() -> i64 { crate::value::I53_MAX }
pub fn is_int_floor(min: &i64) -> bool { *min <= int_floor() }
pub fn is_int_ceiling(max: &i64) -> bool { *max >= int_ceiling() }
/// An int range some i53 value satisfies, with both ends inside i53.
pub fn int_range_valid(min: i64, max: i64) -> bool { int_floor() <= min && min <= max && max <= int_ceiling() }
/// A stored end as written: `None` when it is the i53 limit.
pub fn int_bound(end: i64, limit: i64) -> Option<i64> { (end != limit).then_some(end) }

/// Whether `value` lies inside an inclusive optional range.
pub fn int_in_range(value: i64, min: Option<i64>, max: Option<i64>) -> bool {
    min.is_none_or(|min| value >= min) && max.is_none_or(|max| value <= max)
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Index {
    pub components: Vec<IndexComponent>,
    pub unique: bool,
}

impl Index {
    pub fn of(columns: &[&str], unique: bool) -> Self {
        Self { components: columns.iter().map(|c| IndexComponent::Column((*c).to_owned())).collect(), unique }
    }

    /// Ordered key names: columns and time-function names. A `words(..)`
    /// component contributes nothing here; `word_component` names it.
    /// Use `IndexComponent::column` for actual row fields (such as rule pins).
    pub fn columns(&self) -> Vec<String> {
        self.components.iter().filter_map(|c| c.key_name().cloned()).collect()
    }

    pub fn is_plain(&self) -> bool {
        self.components.iter().all(|c| matches!(c, IndexComponent::Column(_)))
    }

    pub fn uses_column(&self, name: &str) -> bool {
        self.components.iter().any(|component| match component {
            IndexComponent::Column(column) => column == name,
            IndexComponent::Calendar(key) => key.column == name,
            IndexComponent::WordPrefixes { fields, .. } => fields.iter().any(|field| field == name),
            IndexComponent::Contribution(maintain) => maintain.dependencies().contains(name),
        })
    }

    /// The `words(..)` component, if any: its position among the
    /// components and the text columns it tokenizes.
    pub fn word_component(&self) -> Option<(usize, &[String])> {
        self.components.iter().enumerate().find_map(|(position, component)| match component {
            IndexComponent::WordPrefixes { fields, .. } => Some((position, fields.as_slice())),
            IndexComponent::Column(_) | IndexComponent::Calendar(_) | IndexComponent::Contribution(_) => None,
        })
    }

    /// The columns after the first `components` components: a cursor's
    /// suffix, and the order a page continues in. Counted in components,
    /// so a scan whose prefix ends with a word skips the word too.
    pub fn suffix_after(&self, components: usize) -> Vec<String> {
        self.components.iter().skip(components).filter_map(|c| c.key_name().cloned()).collect()
//! Immutable release custody: digest the required tree and flush it before DB selection.
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#5-hot-activation-and-ownership — source durability precedes the database's release pointer.
use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};
use std::{
    fs,
    io::{Read, Write},
    path::{Path, PathBuf},
};

fn files(root: &Path, path: &Path, out: &mut Vec<PathBuf>) -> Result<(), String> {
    let mut entries = fs::read_dir(path)
        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?
        .map(|e| e.map(|e| e.path()).map_err(|e| e.to_string()))
        .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, _>>()?;
    entries.sort();
    for path in entries {
        let relative = path.strip_prefix(root).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        if relative
            .components()
            .next()
            .is_some_and(|c| c.as_os_str() == ".snapback4")
        {
            continue;
        }
        let m = fs::symlink_metadata(&path).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        if m.is_dir() {
            files(root, &path, out)?;
        } else if m.is_file() {
            out.push(path);
        } else {
            return Err(format!(
                "immutable release artifact must be a real directory or regular file: {}",
                path.display()
            ));
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}
pub(crate) fn digest(root: &Path) -> Result<String, String> {
    let mut paths = Vec::new();
    files(root, root, &mut paths)?;
    let mut hash = Sha256::new();
    for path in paths {
        let name = path.strip_prefix(root).unwrap().to_string_lossy();
        // @ref LLP 3000.000.000.000.000#4-cli-surface-contracts — metadata does not change executable release identity.
        if name == ".pending-adoption" || name == "provenance.json" {continue;}
        hash.update((name.len() as u64).to_le_bytes());
        hash.update(name.as_bytes());
        #[cfg(unix)]
        use std::os::unix::fs::OpenOptionsExt;
        let mut options = fs::OpenOptions::new();
        options.read(true);
        #[cfg(unix)]
        options.custom_flags(libc::O_NOFOLLOW);
        let mut file = options.open(&path).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        hash.update(
            file.metadata()
                .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?
                .len()
                .to_le_bytes(),
        );
        let mut buffer = [0u8; 16384];
        loop {
            let n = file.read(&mut buffer).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            if n == 0 {
                break;
            }
            hash.update(&buffer[..n]);
        }
    }
    Ok(format!("{:x}", hash.finalize()))
}
pub(crate) fn durable(root: &Path) -> Result<(), String> {
    let mut paths = Vec::new();
    files(root, root, &mut paths)?;
    for path in paths {
        fs::File::open(path)
            .and_then(|f| f.sync_all())
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    }
    fn dirs(path: &Path) -> Result<(), String> {
        for e in fs::read_dir(path).map_err(|e| e.to_string())? {
            let e = e.map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            if e.file_type().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?.is_dir() {
                dirs(&e.path())?;
            }
        }
        fs::File::open(path)
            .and_then(|f| f.sync_all())
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())
    }
    dirs(root)
}

pub(crate) fn link_store(root: &Path) -> Result<(), String> {
    let project = crate::project::Project { root: root.into() };
    snapback4_core::private_fs::directory(&project.data_dir()).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let path = project.store_path();
    #[cfg(unix)]
    {
        let target = Path::new("../../../store.sqlite");
        match fs::symlink_metadata(&path) {
            Ok(m) if m.file_type().is_symlink() => {
                if fs::read_link(&path).map_err(|e| e.to_string())? != target {
                    fs::remove_file(&path).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
                    std::os::unix::fs::symlink(target, &path).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
                }
            }
            Ok(_) => {
                return Err(format!(
                    "release store path must be a symbolic link: {}",
                    path.display()
                ));
            }
            Err(e) if e.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => {
                std::os::unix::fs::symlink(target, &path).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?
            }
            Err(e) => return Err(e.to_string()),
        }
        Ok(())
    }
    #[cfg(not(unix))]
    Err("serving shared releases requires Unix symbolic links".into())
}

pub(crate) struct Pointer {
    staged: PathBuf,
    dir: PathBuf,
    release: u64,
    previous: Option<u64>,
    rollback: bool,
    ledger: fs::File,
}
impl Pointer {
    pub(crate) fn prepare(
        dir: &Path,
        release: u64,
        previous: Option<u64>,
        rollback: bool,
    ) -> Result<Self, String> {
        let pointer = dir.join("current");
        if pointer.exists() && !pointer.is_file() {
            return Err("current must be a regular release pointer file".into());
        }
        let staged = dir.join(format!(".current-{}-{release}", std::process::id()));
        let file = snapback4_core::private_fs::file(&staged, true).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let mut prepared = Self {
            staged,
            dir: dir.into(),
            release,
            previous,
            rollback,
            ledger: file,
        };
        writeln!(prepared.ledger, "{release}")
            .and_then(|_| prepared.ledger.sync_all())
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        prepared.ledger = fs::OpenOptions::new()
            .create(true)
            .append(true)
            .open(dir.join("releases.jsonl"))
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        writeln!(prepared.ledger,"{}",serde_json::json!({"at":snapback4_core::now_ms(),"release":release,"previous":previous,"rollback":rollback,"state":"prepared"})).and_then(|_|prepared.ledger.sync_all()).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
        Ok(prepared)
    }
    pub(crate) fn finish(&mut self, generation: u64) -> Result<(), String> {
        fs::rename(&self.staged,self.dir.join("current")).and_then(|_|fs::File::open(&self.dir)?.sync_all()).map_err(|e|format!("release {} committed in database, but current copy failed: {e}; inspect `snapback4 releases {}`",self.release,self.dir.display()))?;
        writeln!(self.ledger,"{}",serde_json::json!({"at":snapback4_core::now_ms(),"release":self.release,"previous":self.previous,"rollback":self.rollback,"generation":generation,"state":"activated"})).and_then(|_|self.ledger.sync_all()).map_err(|e|format!("release committed, but ledger completion failed: {e}"))
    }
}
impl Drop for Pointer {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        if self.staged.exists() {
            let _ = fs::remove_file(&self.staged);
        }
    }
}

This proof assumes v1 declares `readItem(item)` returning `items[item]` and `editItem(item)` with `row = items[item]! else NOT_FOUND`; v2 adds `newOnly` and writes an enum value v1 cannot hold. Keep the bearer session in `headers`:

```js
assert.equal(absent.status, 404); assert.equal((await absent.json()).denied.code, 'E_OP');
assert.equal((await post('/m/editItem', {item})).why.code, 'NOT_FOUND');
assert.equal((await post('/q/readItem', {item})).data, null);
await cli('rollback', deployment, '--to', 'latest'); assert.equal((await post('/q/readItem', {item})).data.status, 'new');
```
Previews lead with removed guards and their refusal codes, then group query/count active-table losses, linked dormant rows and safety dependencies per change. Verify filtered/paged/per-viewer results and denominators. Literal-default backfills print `table.column ← value on N rows` in previews and cold/hot deploy receipts: interpreted historical fields, not physical writes. Preview shows old and new rule clauses and mutation `require`s side by side, preserving Boolean parentheses. A proved tightening is a note, including `require A else X` becoming `require A else X; require B else Y` when A's refusal contract is unchanged; changed codes/messages still warn. A loosening or unproved implication is a WARNING, including rollback that removes a new guard. Unchanged clauses and consulted keys, fields, rules, auth and visibility are silent. Unread column domains and forward domain widening do not unprove a probe; narrowing still gets exact dormant-value/population warnings. Changed dependencies group once: verify refusal cases. Comparison never changes admission. Missing operations/schedules, populated removed tables, changed clauses and job bodies are named separately. An unchanged job compiling differently after a dependency change gets a note. Original read-through captures restore only with the same capture definition; new uncaptured rows still need safe audience correspondence. Receipts and previews count storage by kind: `1 row, 2 cells, 0 index entries dormant`. Dormant rows cover their retained cells; standalone cell counts/samples belong only to active rows. Bytes/assets follow the same disjoint ownership. Samples are labelled and unique within each kind/path, for example `rows notes [A]; cells notes.color [B, C]`. A retained index definition represents logical entries to rebuild, not a dormant physical index. A widening note describes values later writes could use; a compatibility WARNING counts only current values the selected release cannot hold. Compatible forward has no such warning.

## Clients across rollback
Save versions/v1/schema.q (and any other .q modules with their original names) outside snapback/ and create `snapback/client-compatibility.json` containing `{"baseline":"versions/v1","since_release":2}`. `check`/`dev` emit added row/result fields as optional, labelled "Since release 2", retaining older result value domains. Required operation parameters remain required; only declared optional, defaulted or deprecated arguments may be omitted. Baseline generation also warns about old-client input breaks and result/refusal changes. This is your release label, not server generation; ordinary types stay required without opt-in. Use `row.color ?? "none"` for the baseline fallback. Keep contemporary fixtures in versions/v1/fixtures/; deploy with `--schema versions/v1` and test with `--source versions/v1` (guide testing). Baselines resolve from the original project root, including saved-source deploys.

`useRelease()` from snapback4/react follows the authenticated catalog even when query gates are closed: loading, denied, or `{generation, operations, tables}` with public argument/field shapes. Generated `api.op.available(release)` is false while unready or when name/kind/existing-argument signatures differ or the server adds a required argument; extra optional served parameters are compatible. This checks capability, not authorization or unchanged business semantics; still handle E_OP/E_INPUT and authored guard refusals when a switch races it. The SDK withdraws old metadata on observed generations, polls while subscribed, and returns loading offline/on catalog failure; account retirement/close withdraws it. Keep draft state above generation-sensitive read/action gates; remount only the gated children. Dormant means unavailable on this release, not deleted. Test v2 → v1 → v2 with one frontend: field fallbacks, missing operations, retained drafts and restored values. Alternatively check selected immutable source in a separate copy and rebuild its matching frontend. Preserve deployment source; Expo/Exact runtime changes also require a device rebuild.

## Trigger a deployed job
A running serve accepts `POST /jobs/<job>` with a full service token; the job executes as the system on its FIFO queue and server clock. Every full service seat can trigger system jobs, so provision these tokens only to trusted operators. Guest/account sessions, read-scoped tokens and personas cannot use this door. `POST /dev/jobs/<job>` remains development-only.
```sh
export SNAPBACK4_SERVICE_TOKEN=$(bunx snapback4 token --service ops --release ./deployment)
bunx snapback4 run reconcile --release ./deployment --json
unset SNAPBACK4_SERVICE_TOKEN
```
`--token-env NAME` reads another environment variable. The command uses served source, so it also works outside the app directory. A lost reply is ambiguous; inspect the queue before retrying. Starter/client recipes: `guide client`.

## Persona transition checklist
The complete account directory, linked membership and real-account transition is `guide production`.
1. Add password identity and signup/login UI; persist sessions with localStorageSessionStore on web or the secure native store, and verify restart.
2. Replace hard-coded persona owners/UI with signed-in account principals; map roles through the trusted seat recipe.
3. Give profiles a production write path and authorized directory; expose no credentials.
4. Create required teams/memberships, invitations or principal sharing; wait for scope delivery before offline reads; test expiry, revocation and outsiders.
5. Bootstrap every required table without fixtures/seed; guide start/client covers first writers and password starters.
6. Prove real-account writes, role checks, sign-out and rollback on durable storage behind TLS.

## Inspection, proxies and TLS
| Door | `dev` | `serve` |
|---|---|---|
| `/q`, `/m`, `GET /` compatibility record | Loopback persona or bearer | Bearer session required, including public rows |
| `/auth/config`; signup/login/guest | Public config; declared sign-in methods | Public config; declared sign-in methods |
| `/seed`, `/dev/*` | Development only | Refused |
| `/jobs/<job>` | Service seat | Full service seat |

Public visitors obtain guest sessions with `use identity`; `use identity password` disables guests (`guide rule-recipes`).

`snapback4 sql 'SELECT id, archived FROM notes' --release ./deployment --json` returns positional rows under `columns`; `--json-objects` returns object rows with direct boolean columns as true/false. Schema-qualified relations, recursive or shadowing/forward-reference CTEs need positional --json. Inspection bypasses read rules and can show expired stored rows; it is an operator tool, not an app query surface. Bind loopback behind a TLS proxy. Forward Authorization and preserve response bodies; allow long polls without buffering or early timeouts. Non-loopback HTTP requires `--i-understand-cleartext` and a trusted private hop behind TLS. Identity rate keys use the socket peer, not forwarded headers. Serve refuses personas and dev doors even through a local proxy; see SERVER.md for capacity. `--json-objects` returns `{header, columns, rows, limit, more, ruleFiltered:false}` with object `rows`; `--json` uses positional `rows`. Put flags outside the quoted SQL.

## Authoring and proof refusals
N_NO_PRODUCTION_WRITER means no production insert path can bootstrap this table. It groups root blockers with their dependents; persona-only schemas get one identity note. Add a service-seat insert mutation and provision real account relationships. E_UPGRADE means installation or post-upgrade check failed. Repair the package-manager or registry error and rerun upgrade. Packages may already be pinned: if check failed, repair the reported schema/device refusal and rerun check.

## Capture a saved version
After check and business cases pass, from the app root:
```sh
snapback4 deploy ./deployment --save-version versions/v1
snapback4 rollback ./deployment --to 1 --check --bridge ./bridge
snapback4 deploy ./deployment --schema ./bridge --check
```

The new versions/v1 captures selected modules, their contemporary fixtures and `versions/v1/release.json` with the actual immutable release id, source hash and CLI version. Unchanged deploy keeps its id; existing snapshots never change. Choose a new positive-numbered versions/vN; saving cannot combine with --check. Saved-source deploy uses its own fixtures. Use the recorded id when choosing a baseline; since_release remains the author-supplied newer-schema label. The bridge starts from complete current Quarry source and this app's `bridge/ROLLBACK-CHECKLIST.md`. Retain current domains, audiences, guards and quotas; repair records through authorized mutations; prove blocked cases refuse and affected lists/counts remain correct; repeat the old-source preview. No bridge is activated or business migration synthesized. JSON includes checklist; --verbose retains full evidence/index paths. Dormant rows reserve ids but no unique slots: new active rows may block restoration. A widened non-unique bridge permits repair before reinstating uniqueness; retained storage cannot supply absent safety logic.

## Keep v1 clients working

`deploy --check --json` reports `clientCompatibility` separately from data
adoption: missing operations, changed kinds, required new arguments, removed or
narrowed inputs, result shape/value changes and changed refusal codes/messages.
Deploy prints the same warnings. Input breaks name the old release and the
E_INPUT/E_OP outcome. A result or behavior review asks you to verify the intended
contract; a correct new privacy denial is intentional behavior, not an input
shape failure. The comparison is conservative, not a proof of unchanged logic.

For example, save this v1 source and its callers sending
`{name:"Ada",obsolete:"old input"}` to `greet`:

```quarry
use identity guests
query greet(name: text <=40, obsolete: text <=40):
  return {message: name + '!'}
```

Deploy this v2 source. The unchanged v1 call still returns `{message:"Ada!"}`;
new callers can use `greeting` and omit `suffix` and `obsolete`:

```quarry
use identity guests
query greeting(name: text <=40, suffix: text <=1 default '!', obsolete: text <=40 deprecated):
  return {message: name + suffix}
alias greet = greeting
```

A literal default handles omitted or null arguments and must fit the declared
scalar type; explicit non-null input still validates. `deprecated` accepts a
validated optional argument but forbids referring to it in the body. Unknown
arguments still refuse. These modifiers apply to non-native Quarry queries and
mutations; helpers, actions and jobs keep their distinct invocation contracts.
Aliases target an authored query/mutation in the same module, carry the same
rules/guards/results, and cannot chain. Retain the old endpoint until its callers
have retired; preserve the old result projection and handle intended denials.

Deploy also warns when later rollback can refuse or hide rows: dropping a
required old column or widening a value domain can strand newly written rows.
The preflight separately attempts exact reverse adoption on its post-deploy
snapshot, naming present-data failures. Keep old fields and compatible domains
in an expand/contract bridge; after writes run the named
`rollback <deployment> --to N --check --bridge <new-dir>` before selecting N.
A green snapshot never freezes later writes.

 # Snapback 4 behavior changes
 
+## 0.4.34
+
+- Guide changes: `guide deploy` explains retained-source reactivation with the same release id, atomic refused-candidate cleanup, the shared `--restore-dormant` intent and deploy-time rollback foresight with bridge commands. Old-client compatibility reports compare operation inputs, results and refusals; required new inputs remain required in generated types. The executable v1/v2 recipe uses literal query/mutation defaults, ignored deprecated inputs and same-module operation aliases. Availability accepts extra optional served arguments.
+
 ## 0.4.33
 
 - Declare independent security invariants beside access rules. `check` reports proved, refuted with a replayable counterexample, or unproved. Supported local rule implications, typed aggregate contributor audiences and exact server lifetime admission have scoped proofs; general temporal histories, opaque writers, capture/live-parent reductions and disconnected-device freshness remain explicitly unproved. Passing finite challenges is not a universal security proof.
